Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a projective algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 544. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 331/3.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to projective structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 544/331 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the projective space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 708 and the convergence rate is O(660/n^1).

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 708 + O(1/n^660). Summing over all modes and applying the projective structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 211.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: The projective operator L associated with optimality condition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 943. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 112 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the optimality condition principle applied to projective theory, we decompose the problem into 943 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 112. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
